# Open-source builds (ROADMAP M8)

M8 is robustness: real open-source C++ projects that already build with
clang are built with kiln as the C++ compiler, through each project's own
build system and test suite, and every failure becomes a reduced
`tests/conformance` case plus a fix. The exit criteria are in
docs/ROADMAP.md M8; the harness contract is spec/landed/harness/WP-666.md.
This page is the index: the per-project sections between the
`<!-- oss:... -->` markers are generated by `oss-report.py --update`, not
hand-edited. Per-project working notes live in `docs/status/oss/<project>.md`.

## Running the harness

```sh
source scripts/env.sh                         # shared jobserver for make
cargo build --profile harness -p kiln_driver  # $CARGO_TARGET_DIR/harness/kiln++
scripts/oss/oss-build.sh googletest --compiler clang   # control build
scripts/oss/oss-build.sh googletest                    # kiln build
scripts/oss/oss-report.py googletest                   # table + clusters
scripts/oss/oss-report.py --update docs/status/oss.md googletest
```

Projects and pinned tags: `scripts/oss/projects.toml`. Checkouts and build
trees go to `.oss/` (gitignored); each build writes
`.oss/build/<project>/<kiln|clang>/{report.json,compile.jsonl,*.log}`.
Every compiler invocation runs under `ulimit -v 3000000` and `timeout 600`
(`scripts/oss/kiln-cxx`); `make` draws from the box's shared jobserver.
A project with `deps` in the toml (re2 needs abseil, googletest and
benchmark) first builds and installs each dependency with the same
compiler into `.oss/<dep>-install-<compiler>/` (once; delete the directory
to rebuild) and finds them through `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH`; dependency TUs are
logged under `.oss/build/<dep>/<compiler>-dep/` and never counted for the
dependent (spec/landed/harness/WP-666.md "Dependencies").
`scripts/oss/selftest.sh` checks the scripts. In a repro command `$KILN` is
the kiln checkout the build ran in, and `kiln++` the binary it used.

The loop:

1. **Build**: control build with clang, then kiln (`oss-build.sh`).
2. **Cluster**: `oss-report.py` groups kiln's failed TUs by normalized
   diagnostic signature; the largest cluster first.
3. **Reduce + WP**: reduce the representative TU to a standalone
   `tests/conformance/<section>/` case, open a work package, fix, re-run.

## Constexpr performance (WP-792)

Wave 4 is the constexpr-heavy projects. Each was built with kiln and
with the control compiler on the same host, once each, and compared per
TU over the TUs that compile under both (`oss-report.py --perf`,
spec/landed/harness/WP-792.md). Time is the TU's CPU time (user plus
system). RSS is its peak, which includes the LLVM backend. A ratio below
1 means kiln is faster or smaller.

| project | control | TUs | time (geomean) | RSS (geomean) | Σ CPU kiln / control | peak TU RSS kiln / control | TUs over 1.2x |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ctre` | clang-19 | 25 | 1.06x | 0.45x | 28 s / 30 s | 166 / 414 MB | 0 of 13 |
| `mp_units` | gcc-16 | 64 | 0.92x | 0.39x | 235 s / 267 s | 573 / 1539 MB | 3 of 61 |
| `daw_json_link` | clang-19 | 159 | 1.06x | 0.41x | 351 s / 343 s | 137 / 304 MB | 3 of 152 |
| `glaze` | clang-19 | 87 | 0.97x | 0.47x | 869 s / 970 s | 1658 / 2171 MB | 2 of 81 |
| `scnlib` | clang-19 | 47 | 0.97x | 0.43x | 188 s / 190 s | 717 / 827 MB | 0 of 41 |
| `reflect_cpp` | clang-19 | 317 | 0.91x | 0.39x | 804 s / 884 s | 250 / 549 MB | 0 of 315 |

- RSS is 0.39x to 0.47x of the control's in every project.
- The heaviest TUs are kiln's best ratios: glaze's `json_test.cpp` is
  0.72x time and 0.57x RSS (103 s vs 144 s), and mp_units'
  `fmt_test.cpp` is 0.69x time and 0.31x RSS against gcc-16.
- The geomean time is above 1 for ctre and daw_json_link, and it comes
  from their many small TUs of 1 to 3 s.
- All eight TUs over 1.2x are on time, never on RSS, and all are 1.20x
  to 1.31x. They are filed as perf clusters on the project pages
  (mp_units P1, daw_json_link P1, glaze P1).
- mp_units is compared with g++-16, because clang 19 cannot build
  mp-units v2.5.0.

g++-16 on the five heaviest TUs of each clang-controlled project. Each
TU was re-run alone with kiln, clang++-19 and g++-16 with the control's
command line (clang-only `-W` flags dropped for gcc), under the
harness's 3 GB address-space cap. Geomean kiln/g++-16:

| project | time | RSS | note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ctre` | 1.21x | 0.29x | g++-16 is the fastest of the three on ctre (2.0 to 3.0 s per TU) |
| `daw_json_link` | 0.49x | 0.20x | |
| `scnlib` | 0.66x | 0.57x | |
| `glaze` | 0.56x | 0.28x | over 2 TUs. g++-16 runs out of the 3 GB cap ("virtual memory exhausted") on `json_test`, `yaml_test` and `beve_test`, where kiln peaks at 1244, 1658 and 759 MB |
| `reflect_cpp` | 0.68x | 0.21x | |

### benchmark (v1.9.5)

| | kiln | clang-19 |
|---|---|---|
| configure | ok | ok |
| build | ok | ok |
| TUs ok / failed | 73 / 0 | 73 / 0 |
| links ok / failed | 49 / 0 | 49 / 0 |

| wall | 31 s | 27 s |
| peak TU RSS | 146 MB | 221 MB |

Failure clusters (kiln): none.
<!-- /oss:benchmark -->

<!-- oss:googletest -->
### googletest (v1.17.0)

| | kiln | clang-19 |
|---|---|---|
| configure | ok | ok |
| build | ok | ok |
| TUs ok / failed | 85 / 0 | 85 / 0 |
| links ok / failed | 67 / 0 | 67 / 0 |
| tests passed / total | 63 / 63 | 63 / 63 |
| wall | 53 s | 100 s |
| peak TU RSS | 668 MB | 794 MB |

Failure clusters (kiln): none.
<!-- /oss:googletest -->

Reduced repros (WP-666, kiln at main f3751eadd):

- Cluster 1, `-W`: kiln++ rejects clang's old spelling of `-Wextra`
  (`kiln++ -W -c x.cpp`; clang accepts it). googletest's
  `cxx_strict_flags` (`googletest/cmake/internal_utils.cmake:97`) passes
  it to the strict-flag targets, `libgtest` among them, so `libgtest`
  never builds and 61 of the 63 tests have no binary.
- Masked behind cluster 1 (found by an exploratory run with a shim mapping
  `-W` to `-Wextra`: 84/85 TUs and 62/63 tests then pass): libstdc++ 14
  `std::get` on a reference to a `std::tuple` that was not instantiated
  earlier in the TU fails deduction (`tuple:2452: no matching function for
  call to '__get_helper'`, candidate note "could not match '_Tuple_impl'
  against 'std::tuple'"): the base classes of the argument's class are not
  considered because the class template specialization is not completed
  first ([temp.deduct.call]/4.3, [temp.inst]/2). Repro, at any `-std`:

  ```cpp
  #include <tuple>
  int g(std::tuple<int, int>& a) { return std::get<0>(a); }
  ```

  A `std::tuple<int, int> x;` before `g` makes it compile; `-stdlib=libc++`
  is unaffected. googletest TU: `googlemock/test/gmock-actions_test.cc`.

Both are fixed by WP-682, which brought googletest to parity with the
clang build. Its working notes are in `docs/status/oss/googletest.md`.

### eigen (3.4.1)

| | kiln | clang-19 |
|---|---|---|
| configure | ok | ok |
| build | rc 2 | rc 2 |
| TUs ok / failed | 539 / 0 | 539 / 0 |

| wall | 1226 s | 1073 s |
| peak TU RSS | 797 MB | 1408 MB |

Failure clusters (kiln): 1.

1. **4x** `link: undefined reference to Q`
   - e.g. `link`: `dynalloc.cpp:(.text+0x1550): undefined reference to `Eigen::PlainObjectBase<Eigen::Matrix<float, 2, 1, 0, 2, 1> >::operator delete[](void*)'`
   - repro: `cd $KILN/.oss/build/eigen/kiln/test && kiln++ -std=c++11 -pedantic -Wall -Wextra -Wundef -Wcast-align -Wchar-subscripts -Wnon-virtual-dtor -Wunused-local-typedefs -Wpointer-arith -Wwrite-strings -Wformat-security -Wshorten-64-to-32 -Wenum-conversion -Wc++11-extensions -Wdouble-promotion -Wshadow -Wno-psabi -Wno-variadic-macros -Wno-long-long -fno-common -fstrict-aliasing -O3 -DNDEBUG -Xlinker --dependency-file=CMakeFiles/dynalloc.dir/link.d CMakeFiles/dynalloc.dir/dynalloc.cpp.o -o dynalloc`

kiln test run: `99% tests passed, 4 tests failed out of 539`

A 132-test subset (539 TUs). The link cluster is the class-template
`operator new`/`delete` instantiation that WP-686 fixed on main; with
it, all 539 tests pass (WP-709, docs/status/oss/eigen.md).

With the WP-686 allocation fixes now on main (#324), the 4 targets that failed to link on the WP-709 branch build and pass: 539/539 tests, at parity with clang-19.

## libc++ results (WP-694)

The wave-1 projects built with `oss-build.sh <project> --stdlib libc++`
under kiln++ and the clang-19 control, both against the system libc++ 19
(`libc++-19-dev`); the `libc++` column of the projects table and the
`:libcxx` blocks below come from `oss-report.py --stdlib libc++ --update`.
All ten are at parity with the control. The first kiln build had two
kiln-only clusters, both fixed in WP-694:

- fmt `test/ranges-test.cc` (2 TUs): "call to consteval function
  `fmt::fstring<tuple_join_view<…>>` is not a constant expression". Under
  `-std=gnu++20` the implicit default constructor of a class with a
  libc++ `std::tuple` member was not constexpr: the tuple's default
  constructor is a constexpr constructor template, which kiln did not
  count as a constexpr default constructor. Test:
  `tests/conformance/class.default.ctor/constexpr-ctor-template.cpp`.
- googletest `googlemock/test/gmock-actions_test.cc` (1 TU): "cannot form
  a reference to 'void'" in libc++ `<string>`. kiln deduced libc++'s
  explicit `basic_string(const T&)` template as a candidate of a
  copy-initialization (a `void` argument passed to a `std::string`
  parameter inside a SFINAE test); an explicit constructor template is
  not a candidate there and is not deduced. Test:
  `tests/conformance/over.match.copy/explicit-ctor-template-not-deduced.cpp`.

range-v3's two failures fail the same way with clang: `test/view/tokenize.cpp`
(libc++'s `std::regex_token_iterator` is not a forward iterator either) and
the `calendar` example, which links the system Boost static libraries that
were built against libstdc++. The harness entry for range-v3 now passes
`-DRANGES_ENABLE_WERROR=OFF -DCMAKE_CXX_FLAGS=-Wno-deprecated-ofast`, as
the WP-669 manual build did; without them the clang control fails every TU
on clang 19's `-Ofast` deprecation under range-v3's `-Werror`.

**Perf finding: file-system syscalls (WP-694).** Under libc++ kiln's
per-TU wall time was above clang's (median ratio 1.1 to 1.9 per project),
although its CPU time was close and its RSS 0.4 to 0.8 times clang's. The
gap is waiting, not computing: on the workspace bind mount (a virtual file
system) kiln blocks about 3000 times per TU against clang's about 600, and
with the same sources copied to the container's own file system the two
take the same time. Likely cause: `fs::canonicalize` on every opened file
(`crates/kiln_source/src/manager.rs:447`, one `lstat` per path component)
and the uncached `metadata` probe per include directory
(`crates/kiln_pp/src/search.rs:309-325`). Reproduce with catch2 `v3.16.1`,
TU `tests/SelfTest/UsageTests/Generators.tests.cpp`, from
`$KILN/.oss/build/catch2/kiln-libcxx/tests`, run under
`/usr/bin/time -f '%e s %U u %S s %w waits %M KB'` with `<cc>` the
harness `kiln++` or `clang++-19` (the same command for both; `-o x.o`
instead of `-fsyntax-only` for the `-c -O3` row):
`<cc> -stdlib=libc++ -I$KILN/.oss/src/catch2/tests/SelfTest -I$KILN/.oss/src/catch2/src/catch2/.. -I$KILN/.oss/build/catch2/kiln-libcxx/generated-includes -O3 -DNDEBUG -std=gnu++20 -Wabsolute-value -Wall -Wcall-to-pure-virtual-from-ctor-dtor -Wcast-align -Wdangling -Wdeprecated -Wdeprecated-register -Wexceptions -Wexit-time-destructors -Wextra -Wextra-semi -Wfloat-equal -Wglobal-constructors -Winit-self -Wmisleading-indentation -Wmismatched-new-delete -Wmismatched-return-types -Wmismatched-tags -Wmissing-braces -Wmissing-declarations -Wmissing-noreturn -Wmissing-prototypes -Wmissing-variable-declarations -Wnon-virtual-dtor -Wnull-dereference -Wold-style-cast -Woverloaded-virtual -Wparentheses -Wpedantic -Wredundant-decls -Wreorder -Wreturn-std-move -Wshadow -Wstrict-aliasing -Wsuggest-destructor-override -Wsuggest-override -Wundef -Wuninitialized -Wunneeded-internal-declaration -Wunreachable-code-aggressive -Wunused -Wunused-function -Wunused-parameter -Wvla -Wweak-vtables -Wno-missing-noreturn -fsyntax-only $KILN/.oss/src/catch2/tests/SelfTest/UsageTests/Generators.tests.cpp`.
For the last row the catch2 source tree and `generated-includes` were
copied under `/tmp` and the `-I` paths pointed there.

| where the sources are | kiln++ | clang++-19 |
|---|---|---|
| workspace bind mount, `-fsyntax-only` | 2.84 s wall, 1.62 s user, 3166 waits, 73 MB | 1.85 s wall, 1.43 s user, 577 waits, 206 MB |
| workspace bind mount, `-c -O3` | 3.88 s wall, 2.62 s user, 90 MB | 2.50 s wall, 2.19 s user, 223 MB |
| container file system, `-fsyntax-only` | 1.30 s wall, 1.19 s user, 168 waits | 1.37 s wall, 1.23 s user, 69 waits |

The host load average was 17 to 25 during these runs, so the wall times of
the whole-project rows below are noisy.

1. **1x** `link: (.text+NxN): undefined reference to Q`; 1 also fails with clang
   - e.g. `link`: `(.text+0x108): undefined reference to `std::_Rb_tree_decrement(std::_Rb_tree_node_base*)'`
   - repro: `cd $KILN/.oss/build/range_v3/kiln-libcxx/example && kiln++ -stdlib=libc++ -Wno-deprecated-ofast -O3 -DNDEBUG -Xlinker --dependency-file=CMakeFiles/range.v3.calendar.dir/link.d CMakeFiles/range.v3.calendar.dir/calendar.cpp.o -o range.v3.calendar /usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libboost_date_time.a /usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libboost_program_options.a`
2. **1x** `reject: error: static assertion failed due to requirement Q: Concept assertion failed : forward_range<decltype(rng)>`; 1 also fails with clang
   - e.g. `$KILN/.oss/src/range_v3/test/view/tokenize.cpp`: `$KILN/.oss/src/range_v3/test/view/tokenize.cpp:25:5: error: static assertion failed due to requirement 'static_cast<bool>(forward_range<ranges::tokenize_view<ranges::ref_view<std::string>, std::regex, int>>)': Concept assertion failed : forward_range<decltype(rng)>`
   - repro: `cd $KILN/.oss/build/range_v3/kiln-libcxx/test/view && kiln++ -stdlib=libc++ -I$KILN/.oss/src/range_v3/include -Wno-deprecated-ofast -O3 -DNDEBUG -std=gnu++20 -std=c++2a -Wall -Wextra -D__extern_always_inline=inline -ftemplate-backtrace-limit=0 -fmacro-backtrace-limit=1 -fomit-frame-pointer -Ofast -fstrict-aliasing -ffast-math -fsized-deallocation -march=native -mtune=native -Weverything -pedantic -pedantic-errors -Wno-c++98-compat -Wno-c++98-compat-pedantic -Wno-weak-vtables -Wno-padded -Wno-missing-variable-declarations -Wno-documentation -Wno-documentation-unknown-command -Wno-old-style-cast -Wno-missing-prototypes -MD -MT test/view/CMakeFiles/range.v3.view.tokenize.dir/tokenize.cpp.o -MF CMakeFiles/range.v3.view.tokenize.dir/tokenize.cpp.o.d -o CMakeFiles/range.v3.view.tokenize.dir/tokenize.cpp.o -c $KILN/.oss/src/range_v3/test/view/tokenize.cpp`
